---
name: pm-gitlab
description: GitLab connector knowledge and safe action guide.
---

# pm-gitlab

## Purpose

Reads GitLab projects, groups, users, and issues; carries a complete planned official GitLab operation ledger for future typed streams, writes, direct reads, binary transfers, and changefeeds.

## Icon

- asset: icons/gitlab.svg
- source: upstream_registry
- review_status: upstream_seeded
- review_url: https://docs.gitlab.com/ee/api/rest/deprecations.html

## Capabilities

- check=true catalog=true read=true write=false query=false
- Integration type: api

## Authentication

- Use pm credentials add with --from-env or --value-stdin for secret fields.

## Configuration

- base_url
- mode
- page_size
- start_date
- access_token (secret)

## ETL Streams

- projects:
  - primary key: id
  - cursor: last_activity_at
  - fields: archived(), created_at(), default_branch(), description(), forks_count(), id(), last_activity_at(), name(), open_issues_count(), path(), path_with_namespace(), star_count(), visibility(), web_url()
- groups:
  - primary key: id
  - cursor: created_at
  - fields: created_at(), description(), full_name(), full_path(), id(), name(), parent_id(), path(), visibility(), web_url()
- users:
  - primary key: id
  - cursor: created_at
  - fields: bot(), created_at(), id(), is_admin(), name(), state(), username(), web_url()
- issues:
  - primary key: id
  - cursor: updated_at
  - fields: author_id(), closed_at(), created_at(), downvotes(), id(), iid(), project_id(), state(), title(), updated_at(), upvotes(), user_notes_count(), web_url()

## Sync Modes

- ETL sync modes: full_refresh_append, full_refresh_overwrite, full_refresh_overwrite_deduped

## Security

- read risk: external GitLab API reads for the four implemented streams; planned reads remain metadata-only until fixture-backed
- write risk: no executable write actions in this wave; planned mutations require reverse ETL plan, preview, explicit approval, and destructive typed confirmation where applicable
- approval: destructive/admin operations are in scope but blocked until connector-local named actions provide confirm=destructive and verified safety evidence
- Never pass secret values in chat, shell arguments, logs, docs, or JSON output.

## Command Surface

- Inspect GitLab streams and planned typed operations without exposing raw API escape hatches.
- Usage: pm gitlab <command> [flags]
- Source CLI: glab / GitLab API (https://gitlab.com/gitlab-org/gitlab/-/raw/9cd04099eb59d87335798e4f57a2bc5a2622e4cc/doc/api/openapi/openapi_v2.yaml)
- Global flags:
  - --credential (string): Credential name to use for a future GitLab command.
  - --connection (string): Alias for --credential.
  - --config (string_array): Connector config override as key=value.
  - --json (boolean): Emit machine-readable JSON output.
  - --limit (integer): Maximum PM ETL records to emit; does not control GitLab provider-side totals.
  - --max-bytes (integer): Maximum future direct-read or binary response bytes; planned operations declare bounded metadata.
  - --preview (boolean): Preview a future reverse-ETL write command without making a network mutation.
  - --approve (string): Approval token required to execute a future reverse-ETL plan.
  - --confirm (string): Typed confirmation challenge required for future destructive reverse-ETL writes.
- Implemented fixture-backed streams
  - projects list - List GitLab projects as ETL records. [intent=etl availability=implemented stream=projects]; notes: Implemented fixture-backed GitLab ETL stream; use --limit for bounded output and saved credentials/config for live reads.
  - groups list - List GitLab groups as ETL records. [intent=etl availability=implemented stream=groups]; notes: Implemented fixture-backed GitLab ETL stream; use --limit for bounded output and saved credentials/config for live reads.
  - users list - List GitLab users as ETL records. [intent=etl availability=implemented stream=users]; notes: Implemented fixture-backed GitLab ETL stream; use --limit for bounded output and saved credentials/config for live reads.
  - issues list - List GitLab issues as ETL records. [intent=etl availability=implemented stream=issues]; notes: Implemented fixture-backed GitLab ETL stream; use --limit for bounded output and saved credentials/config for live reads.

## Commands

### Inspect as a manual

```bash
pm connectors inspect gitlab
```

### Inspect as structured JSON

```bash
pm connectors inspect gitlab --json
```

## Agent Rules

- Run pm connectors inspect gitlab before creating credentials or plans.
- Use --json only when the caller needs structured output; use the manual for human-readable guidance.
- Never ask the user to paste secret values into chat.
